`src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DraftQueueTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.Instant

// especificación de F8 §2.2.17, domain-model §7.4: qué toma JOB-05
class DraftQueueTest {
    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-15T15:00:00Z")
    private val stale = Duration.ofMinutes(15)

    private fun pending(
        status: String?,
        requested: String?,
        generated: String?,
        updated: Instant = now
    ) = DraftQueue.pending(
        mapOf(Deliverables.STATUS to status, Deliverables.REQUESTED_AT to requested, Deliverables.GENERATED_AT to generated),
        updated,
        now,
        stale
    )

    @Test
    fun `un pedido sin borrador o posterior al borrador esta pendiente`() {
        assertTrue(pending(null, "2026-10-15T14:59:00Z", null))
        assertTrue(pending(Deliverables.READY, "2026-10-15T14:59:00Z", "2026-10-15T14:00:00Z"))
        assertTrue(pending(Deliverables.FAILED, "2026-10-15T14:59:00Z", "2026-10-15T14:00:00Z"))
        assertFalse(pending(Deliverables.READY, "2026-10-15T13:59:00Z", "2026-10-15T14:00:00Z"))
        assertFalse(pending(null, null, null))
    }

    @Test
    fun `uno EN COLA o EN PROCESO solo se retoma pasado stale-after`() {
        assertFalse(pending(Deliverables.QUEUED, "2026-10-15T14:59:00Z", null, updated = now.minusSeconds(60)))
        assertTrue(pending(Deliverables.QUEUED, "2026-10-15T14:00:00Z", null, updated = now.minus(Duration.ofMinutes(16))))
        assertFalse(pending(Deliverables.RUNNING, "2026-10-15T14:50:00Z", "2026-10-15T14:55:00Z"))
        assertTrue(pending(Deliverables.RUNNING, "2026-10-15T14:00:00Z", "2026-10-15T14:40:00Z"))
    }
}
```


`src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableDoubles.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import kotlinx.coroutines.reactive.awaitSingle
import kotlinx.coroutines.reactor.asCoroutineContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.TestConfiguration
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DefaultDataBufferFactory
import org.springframework.security.core.context.ReactiveSecurityContextHolder
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.ReactiveJwtDecoder
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.server.resource.authentication.JwtAuthenticationToken
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux
import sgspe.common.Records
import sgspe.privacy.CanonicalJson
import sgspe.privacy.ReportImage
import sgspe.privacy.ReportMapImages
import wasichai.files.FileService
import wasichai.files.FileUpload
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.util.Base64
import java.util.UUID
import javax.imageio.ImageIO

// los dobles de los puertos de JOB-05 en las pruebas de integración de F8 (especificación de F8 §2.2.17; decisión 1 del plan
// 8.2b). un solo contexto de Spring para todas las clases que extienden F8ApiTest:
// - FakePdfConverter: un PDF con Liberation Sans incrustada (y la identificación PDF/A-2b en las candidatas) en lugar de
//   Gotenberg (plan 8.2c); `unavailable` simula Gotenberg caído
// - FakeReportMaps: dos PNG (INTERNO y EXTERNO) en lugar de GeoServer; `failing` simula un mapa fallido
// - PersonDraftFiles: sube y adjunta los siete archivos como la persona de `token` (el admin de la prueba), con la marca de
//   Records, porque wasichai 1.2.0 no deja a la plataforma adjuntar (UPS-31). la Tarea 15 lo quita
class DeliverableDoubles {
    class FakePdfConverter : PdfConverter {
        @Volatile var unavailable = false
        val requests: MutableList<PdfRequest> = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf())

        override suspend fun convert(
            docx: ByteArray,
            request: PdfRequest
        ): ByteArray {
            if (unavailable) throw PdfConverterUnavailable("doble sin conversor")
            requests += request
            return TestPdfs.liberation(request.pdfa, "${request.code} Rev. ${request.revision}")
        }
    }

    class FakeReportMaps : ReportMaps {
        @Volatile var failing = false

        override suspend fun draw(
            organizationId: UUID,
            request: MapRequest
        ): ReportMapImages {
            if (failing) return ReportMapImages()
            return ReportMapImages(image(255), image(128, omitted = true))
        }

        private fun image(
            shade: Int,
            omitted: Boolean = false
        ): ReportImage {
            val png =
                ByteArrayOutputStream()
                    .also { out ->
                        ImageIO.write(BufferedImage(160, 120, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB).apply { setRGB(0, 0, shade) }, "png", out)
                    }.toByteArray()
            return ReportImage(Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(png), CanonicalJson.sha256(png), omitted)
        }
    }

    class PersonDraftFiles(
        private val records: Records,
        private val files: FileService,
        private val decoder: ReactiveJwtDecoder
    ) : DraftFiles {
        @Volatile var token: String? = null

        override suspend fun attach(
            organizationId: UUID,
            deliverableId: UUID,
            files: List<DraftFile>,
            fields: Map<String, Any?>
        ) {
            val bearer = checkNotNull(token) { "PersonDraftFiles sin token: F8ApiTest.generate() lo fija" }
            val jwt = decoder.decode(bearer.removePrefix("Bearer ")).awaitSingle()
            withContext(ReactiveSecurityContextHolder.withAuthentication(JwtAuthenticationToken(jwt)).asCoroutineContext()) {
                val ids =
                    files.associate { file ->
                        val upload = FileUpload(file.fileName, file.contentType, Flux.just(DefaultDataBufferFactory.sharedInstance.wrap(file.bytes)))
                        file.field to
                            this@PersonDraftFiles
                                .files
                                .stage(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, file.field, upload)
                                .id
                                .toString()
                    }
                records.patch(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, deliverableId, fields + ids, DeliverableGenerationJob.REASON)
            }
        }
    }

    @TestConfiguration
    class Config {
        @Bean
        @Primary
        fun fakePdfConverter(): FakePdfConverter = FakePdfConverter()

        @Bean
        @Primary
        fun fakeReportMaps(): FakeReportMaps = FakeReportMaps()

        @Bean
        @Primary
        fun personDraftFiles(
            records: Records,
            files: FileService,
            decoder: ReactiveJwtDecoder
        ): PersonDraftFiles = PersonDraftFiles(records, files, decoder)
    }
}
```


En `F8ApiTest`: la anotación `@Import(DeliverableDoubles.Config::class)` sobre la clase, los cuatro `@Autowired`
(`generationJob`, `fakePdf`, `fakeMaps`, `draftFiles`) y estas ayudas:

```kotlin
    // pide un borrador por su endpoint (POST …/drafts con motivo) como `account`
    protected fun requestDraft(
        deliverable: String,
        account: Account = admin()
    ): Response = exchange("POST", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$deliverable/drafts", null, account.token, withReason("borrador de prueba"))

    // una vuelta de JOB-05; los archivos los adjunta el admin (PersonDraftFiles, UPS-31)
    protected fun generate(): Int {
        draftFiles.token = token
        return runBlocking { generationJob.runOnce() } ?: 0
    }

    // un T2 listo para pedir su borrador: el escenario de reportScenario con su entregable INFORME DE CAMPO Y AVANCE
    protected fun requestedFieldReport(scenario: ReportScenario = reportScenario()): String {
        val response = requestDraft(scenario.fieldReport)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.ACCEPTED, response.status, response.body)
        return scenario.fieldReport
    }
```

`src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableDraftsApiTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import sgspe.F8ApiTest
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit
import java.util.UUID

// especificación de F8 §3.3 y §3.5, §5 (DeliverableDraftsApiTest), deliverables.md §8.2 y §13.3 («Almacenamiento»): el pedido,
// JOB-05 de punta a punta contra wasichai real con los dobles de PDF y mapas, y los siete archivos adjuntos por PersonDraftFiles
// mientras UPS-31 siga abierta (la Tarea 15 los adjunta como la plataforma)
class DeliverableDraftsApiTest : F8ApiTest() {
    private fun codes(response: Response): List<String> = tree(response.body)["errors"].values().map { it["message"].asString() }

    private fun draft(id: String) = tree(send("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft", null, HttpStatus.OK))

    @Test
    fun `un pedido fuera de draft u observed es 409, sin parametros 422 por campo y un T1 todavia no se genera`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val bare = deliverable(project)
        val missing = requestDraft(bare)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT, missing.status, missing.body)
        assertEquals(setOf("community", "project.working_crs"), tree(missing.body)["errors"].values().map { it["field"].asString() }.toSet())
        assertTrue(codes(missing).all { it == "DELIVERABLE_PARAMETERS_MISSING" })
        val t1 = requestDraft(deliverable(project, SOCIAL_DIAGNOSIS))
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT, t1.status, t1.body)
        assertEquals(listOf("TYPE_NOT_AVAILABLE"), codes(t1))
        val approved = approvedDeliverable(project)
        val late = requestDraft(approved)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, late.status, late.body)
        assertEquals(listOf("STATE_MISMATCH"), codes(late))
    }

    @Test
    fun `JOB-05 deja LISTO con los siete archivos y un borrador nuevo los reemplaza con la letra siguiente`() {
        val id = requestedFieldReport()
        assertTrue(draft(id)["pending"].asBoolean())
        assertTrue(generate() >= 1)
        val first = record("deliverable", id)["attributes"]
        assertEquals(Deliverables.READY, first[Deliverables.STATUS].asString(), "$first")
        assertEquals("A", first[Deliverables.DRAFT_REVISION].asString())
        for (field in Deliverables.DRAFT_FILES) assertTrue(first[field]?.has("sha256") == true, field)
        assertEquals(first[Deliverables.MODEL_SHA256].asString(), first[Deliverables.DRAFT_MODEL]["sha256"].asString())
        val status = draft(id)
        assertEquals(false, status["pending"].asBoolean())
        assertEquals(
            first[Deliverables.CANDIDATE_EXTERNAL_PDF]["sha256"].asString(),
            status["levels"]
                .values()
                .single {
                    it["level"].asString() == "EXTERNO"
                }["candidateSha256"]
                .asString()
        )
        assertTrue(fakePdf.requests.any { it.pdfa } && fakePdf.requests.any { !it.pdfa })
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.ACCEPTED, requestDraft(id).status)
        generate()
        val second = record("deliverable", id)["attributes"]
        assertEquals("B", second[Deliverables.DRAFT_REVISION].asString())
        assertNotEquals(first[Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF]["id"].asString(), second[Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF]["id"].asString())
    }

    @Test
    fun `sin conversor queda FALLIDO con PDF_CONVERTER_UNAVAILABLE y un mapa fallido deja LISTO con MAP_FAILED`() {
        val failed = requestedFieldReport()
        fakePdf.unavailable = true
        try {
            generate()
        } finally {
            fakePdf.unavailable = false
        }
        assertEquals(Deliverables.FAILED, record("deliverable", failed)["attributes"][Deliverables.STATUS].asString())
        assertEquals(listOf(Deliverables.PDF_CONVERTER_UNAVAILABLE), draft(failed)["warnings"].values().map { it["code"].asString() })
        val mapless = requestedFieldReport()
        fakeMaps.failing = true
        try {
            generate()
        } finally {
            fakeMaps.failing = false
        }
        assertEquals(Deliverables.READY, record("deliverable", mapless)["attributes"][Deliverables.STATUS].asString())
        val warnings = draft(mapless)["warnings"].values().map { it["code"].asString() to it["section"]?.asString() }
        assertTrue(Deliverables.MAP_FAILED to "portada" in warnings, "$warnings")
    }

    @Test
    fun `un pedido EN PROCESO viejo se retoma y uno reciente no`() {
        val recent = requestedFieldReport()
        platformUpdate("deliverable", recent, mapOf(Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.RUNNING, Deliverables.GENERATED_AT to Instant.now().toString()))
        val old = requestedFieldReport()
        platformUpdate(
            "deliverable",
            old,
            mapOf(
                Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.RUNNING,
                Deliverables.GENERATED_AT to Instant.now().minus(20, ChronoUnit.MINUTES).toString()
            )
        )
        generate()
        assertEquals(Deliverables.RUNNING, record("deliverable", recent)["attributes"][Deliverables.STATUS].asString())
        assertEquals(Deliverables.READY, record("deliverable", old)["attributes"][Deliverables.STATUS].asString())
    }

    @Test
    fun `nadie escribe draft_ ni current_version por la API generica, y reenviar los descriptores leidos no es cambiarlos`() {
        val id = deliverable(testProject())
        readyDraft(id)
        val resent = edit("deliverable", id, mapOf("title" to "Informe de campo y avance corregido"), headers = withReason("título"))
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.OK, resent.status, resent.body)
        assertEquals(Deliverables.READY, record("deliverable", id)["attributes"][Deliverables.STATUS].asString())
        val other = stage(admin(), "deliverable", Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF, PDF, "otro.pdf", "application/pdf")
        val swapped = edit("deliverable", id, mapOf(Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF to other), headers = withReason("cambio"))
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, swapped.status, swapped.body)
        assertEquals(listOf("DERIVED_FIELD"), codes(swapped))
        val status = edit("deliverable", id, mapOf(Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.FAILED), headers = withReason("cambio"))
        assertEquals(listOf("DERIVED_FIELD"), codes(status))
        assertEquals(UUID.fromString(id).toString(), record("deliverable", id)["id"].asString())
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange
import sgspe.common.Callers
import sgspe.common.CommandAnswer
import sgspe.common.Finding
import sgspe.common.Idempotency
import sgspe.common.IdempotencyKey
import sgspe.common.Permissions
import sgspe.common.Records
import sgspe.common.StrictQuery
import sgspe.common.Transactions
import sgspe.common.toCodedException
import wasichai.core.common.Actions
import wasichai.core.common.FieldViolation
import wasichai.core.common.ValidationException
import wasichai.core.data.ChangeReason
import wasichai.core.data.RecordResponse
import java.time.Clock
import java.util.UUID

// el estado del último borrador (api.md §4.12): de cada candidata solo su SHA-256 (nunca se sirve)
data class DraftStatus(
    val status: String?,
    val requestedAt: String?,
    val generatedAt: String?,
    val revision: String?,
    val modelSha256: String?,
    val pending: Boolean,
    val levels: List<DraftLevel>,
    val warnings: List<DraftWarning>
)

data class DraftLevel(
    val level: String,
    val candidateSha256: String?
)

data class DraftWarning(
    val code: String,
    val section: String?
)

// pedir un borrador y leer su estado (especificación de F8 §3.5, deliverables.md §8.2), como la persona: el pedido exige UPDATE
// y GENERATE sobre deliverable, procede solo en draft u observed (409 STATE_MISMATCH), con el tipo disponible (422
// TYPE_NOT_AVAILABLE: T1 llega con el plan 8.2c) y sus parámetros (422 DELIVERABLE_PARAMETERS_MISSING, un error por campo), y
// escribe solo generation_requested_at con la marca de Records y el motivo; JOB-05 lo toma después. todo dentro del candado
// del entregable, que es el de una transición de WF-H que escribe
@Service
class DeliverableDrafts(
    private val records: Records,
    private val callers: Callers,
    private val permissions: Permissions,
    private val transactions: Transactions,
    private val types: DeliverableTypes,
    private val clock: Clock
) {
    suspend fun request(
        id: UUID,
        reason: String?
    ): CommandAnswer {
        if (reason.isNullOrBlank()) throw ValidationException("Pedir un borrador exige un motivo", "reason", "envíe la cabecera X-Change-Reason")
        val user = callers.require()
        permissions.require(
            user,
            "Pedir un borrador",
            Actions.UPDATE to Deliverables.DELIVERABLE,
            Deliverables.GENERATE to Deliverables.DELIVERABLE
        )
        val updated =
            transactions.locked("${Deliverables.DELIVERABLE}:$id") {
                val deliverable = records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id)
                if (deliverable.state !in Deliverables.REQUESTABLE) {
                    throw Finding(
                        "STATE_MISMATCH",
                        "Un borrador se pide en draft u observed; este entregable está en ${deliverable.state}",
                        "record"
                    ).toCodedException()
                }
                val type =
                    types.of(deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.TYPE]?.toString())
                        ?: throw Finding(
                            TYPE_NOT_AVAILABLE,
                            "El tipo de entregable todavía no se genera en esta versión",
                            Deliverables.TYPE,
                            status = HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT
                        ).toCodedException()
                val project = records.get(Deliverables.PROJECT, UUID.fromString(deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.PROJECT].toString()))
                val missing = type.missingParameters(deliverable.attributes, project.attributes)
                if (missing.isNotEmpty()) {
                    throw sgspe.common.RuleException(
                        HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
                        "Faltan parámetros del tipo para pedir un borrador",
                        missing.map { FieldViolation(it, PARAMETERS_MISSING) }
                    )
                }
                records.patch(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id, mapOf(Deliverables.REQUESTED_AT to clock.instant().toString()), reason)
            }
        return CommandAnswer(HttpStatus.ACCEPTED, mapOf("deliverable" to updated), updated.updatedAt, "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft")
    }

    suspend fun status(id: UUID): DraftStatus = status(records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id))

    companion object {
        const val PARAMETERS_MISSING = "DELIVERABLE_PARAMETERS_MISSING"
        const val TYPE_NOT_AVAILABLE = "TYPE_NOT_AVAILABLE"

        fun status(deliverable: RecordResponse): DraftStatus {
            val a = deliverable.attributes
            val status = a[Deliverables.STATUS]?.toString()

            fun sha(field: String): String? = (a[field] as? Map<*, *>)?.get("sha256")?.toString()
            val requested = DraftQueue.instant(a[Deliverables.REQUESTED_AT])
            val generated = DraftQueue.instant(a[Deliverables.GENERATED_AT])
            val pending =
                status == Deliverables.QUEUED || status == Deliverables.RUNNING ||
                    (requested != null && (generated == null || requested.isAfter(generated)))
            return DraftStatus(
                status,
                requested?.toString(),
                generated?.toString(),
                a[Deliverables.DRAFT_REVISION]?.toString(),
                a[Deliverables.MODEL_SHA256]?.toString(),
                pending,
                listOf(
                    DraftLevel(Deliverables.INTERNAL, sha(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_PDF)),
                    DraftLevel(Deliverables.EXTERNAL, sha(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_EXTERNAL_PDF))
                ),
                DraftGeneration.parse(a[Deliverables.WARNINGS]).map { DraftWarning(it.code, it.section) }
            )
        }
    }
}

// api.md §4.12: POST …/drafts (CR, Idempotency-Key) → 202 con Location del estado; GET …/draft → el estado (READ)
@RestController
class DeliverableDraftsController(
    private val drafts: DeliverableDrafts,
    private val idempotency: Idempotency
) {
    @PostMapping("/api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/drafts")
    suspend fun request(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        @RequestHeader(name = ChangeReason.HEADER, required = false) reason: String?,
        @RequestHeader(name = IdempotencyKey.HEADER, required = false) key: String?,
        exchange: ServerWebExchange
    ): ResponseEntity<ByteArray> {
        StrictQuery.requireOnly(exchange.request.queryParams, emptySet())
        return idempotency.respond(key, "POST", exchange.request.path.value(), emptyMap<String, Any?>()) {
            drafts.request(id, ChangeReason.fromHeader(reason))
        }
    }

    @GetMapping("/api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/draft")
    suspend fun status(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        exchange: ServerWebExchange
    ): DraftStatus {
        StrictQuery.requireOnly(exchange.request.queryParams, emptySet())
        return drafts.status(id)
    }
}
```


`src/main/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableGenerationJob.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.EnableScheduling
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component
import sgspe.common.Criteria
import sgspe.common.LIMA
import sgspe.common.Organizations
import sgspe.common.Records
import sgspe.common.StoredFiles
import sgspe.common.StoredRead
import sgspe.common.SweepTiming
import sgspe.privacy.CanonicalJson
import sgspe.privacy.Disclosure
import sgspe.privacy.PersonalDataScan
import sgspe.privacy.ReportDisclosure
import sgspe.privacy.ReportModel
import sgspe.privacy.ReportWarning
import wasichai.core.data.RecordResponse
import wasichai.core.platform.ClusterLock
import java.time.Clock
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.LocalDate
import java.util.UUID

// lo pendiente de JOB-05, en Kotlin sobre los entregables en draft u observed (especificación de F8 §2.2.17, domain-model §7.4):
// un pedido sin borrador, o posterior al borrador; uno EN COLA o EN PROCESO solo si lleva más de stale-after (EN PROCESO
// desde draft_generated_at, el instante de congelar; EN COLA desde la última escritura del registro), porque otra corrida lo
// tiene o se cayó
object DraftQueue {
    fun pending(
        attributes: Map<String, Any?>,
        updatedAt: Instant?,
        now: Instant,
        staleAfter: Duration
    ): Boolean {
        val requested = instant(attributes[Deliverables.REQUESTED_AT]) ?: return false
        val generated = instant(attributes[Deliverables.GENERATED_AT])
        return when (attributes[Deliverables.STATUS]?.toString()) {
            Deliverables.QUEUED -> updatedAt == null || updatedAt.plus(staleAfter).isBefore(now)
            Deliverables.RUNNING -> generated == null || generated.plus(staleAfter).isBefore(now)
            else -> generated == null || requested.isAfter(generated)
        }
    }

    fun instant(raw: Any?): Instant? = raw?.toString()?.let { runCatching { Instant.parse(it) }.getOrNull() }
}

// un borrador que no se pudo terminar: su código va a draft_warnings con FALLIDO
class DraftFailure(
    val code: String,
    val section: String? = null
) : RuntimeException(code)

// un borrador de punta a punta (especificación de F8 §3.3, pasos 1 a 4): EN COLA; lee el entregable, sus secciones, el
// proyecto y la plantilla; ensambla con la clase del tipo, pide los mapas y avisa datos personales; congela el modelo (EN
// PROCESO con draft_generated_at, draft_model_sha256 y draft_revision); vuelve a leer lo congelado y, por nivel, aplica
// ReportDisclosure, escribe el DOCX del borrador y el de la candidata, los convierte y verifica cada PDF; y sube los siete
// archivos con LISTO y los avisos en una sola escritura (DraftFiles). todo error deja FALLIDO con su código. lee y escribe
// como la plataforma (con el origen job:deliverable-generation), nunca aplica transiciones ni escribe secciones. los logs
// llevan ids y códigos, nunca contenido
@Component
class DraftGeneration(
    private val records: Records,
    private val types: DeliverableTypes,
    private val sources: ReportSources,
    private val maps: ReportMaps,
    private val pdf: PdfConverter,
    private val files: DraftFiles,
    private val stored: StoredFiles,
    private val properties: DeliverableProperties,
    private val clock: Clock
) {
    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(javaClass)
    private val writer = DocxWriter(properties.photos)

    suspend fun run(
        organizationId: UUID,
        slug: String?,
        deliverableId: UUID
    ) {
        try {
            val frozen = platform(organizationId) { freeze(organizationId, slug, deliverableId) } ?: return
            val outputs = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { generate(organizationId, frozen) }
            val warnings = frozen.model.warnings
            files.attach(organizationId, deliverableId, outputs, mapOf(Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.READY, Deliverables.WARNINGS to text(warnings)))
            log.info("JOB-05: borrador {} LISTO del entregable {} con {} avisos", frozen.model.meta.draftRevision, deliverableId, warnings.size)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val code =
                when (e) {
                    is DraftFailure -> e.code
                    is PdfConverterUnavailable -> Deliverables.PDF_CONVERTER_UNAVAILABLE
                    else -> Deliverables.GENERATION_FAILED
                }
            if (code == Deliverables.GENERATION_FAILED) log.error("JOB-05: falló el borrador del entregable {}", deliverableId, e)
            log.warn("JOB-05: borrador FALLIDO del entregable {} con {}", deliverableId, code)
            platform(organizationId) {
                records.patch(
                    Deliverables.DELIVERABLE,
                    deliverableId,
                    mapOf(
                        Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.FAILED,
                        Deliverables.WARNINGS to text(listOf(ReportWarning(code, (e as? DraftFailure)?.section)))
                    ),
                    DeliverableGenerationJob.REASON
                )
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun <T> platform(
        organizationId: UUID,
        block: suspend () -> T
    ): T = records.asPlatform(organizationId, DeliverableGenerationJob.SOURCE, block)

    class Frozen(
        val model: ReportModel,
        val bytes: ByteArray,
        val template: ByteArray,
        val author: String?
    )

    // pasos 1 y 2, como la plataforma. null si el entregable ya no está en draft u observed
    private suspend fun freeze(
        organizationId: UUID,
        slug: String?,
        id: UUID
    ): Frozen? {
        val deliverable = records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id)
        if (deliverable.state !in Deliverables.REQUESTABLE) return null
        records.patch(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id, mapOf(Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.QUEUED), DeliverableGenerationJob.REASON)
        val type = types.of(deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.TYPE]?.toString()) ?: throw DraftFailure(DeliverableDrafts.TYPE_NOT_AVAILABLE)
        val template = template(organizationId, deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.TEMPLATE_KEY]?.toString())
        val scheme = properties.revisions.of(slug)
        val previous = deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.DRAFT_REVISION]?.toString()
        val previousAt = DraftQueue.instant(deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.GENERATED_AT])
        val reset = previousAt != null && issuedAfter(id, previousAt)
        val sections =
            records.all(Deliverables.SECTION, mapOf(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE to id.toString())).sortedBy {
                (it.attributes["sort_order"] as? Number)?.toInt()
                    ?: 0
            }
        val input =
            AssemblyInput(
                deliverable = SourceRow(deliverable.id, deliverable.state, deliverable.attributes),
                sections = sections.map { SourceRow(it.id, it.state, it.attributes) },
                templateId = template.id,
                templateSha256 = template.sha256,
                draftRevision = RevisionLabels.nextDraft(previous, reset, scheme),
                issueRevision = RevisionLabels.nextIssue(deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.CURRENT_REVISION]?.toString(), scheme),
                today = LocalDate.now(clock.withZone(LIMA))
            )
        val assembled = type.assemble(input, sources.of(organizationId, type))
        val drawn = assembled.maps.associate { it.key to maps.draw(organizationId, it) }
        val warnings =
            drawn
                .filterValues { it.failed }
                .keys
                .sorted()
                .map { ReportWarning(Deliverables.MAP_FAILED, it) } +
                PersonalDataScan.scan(assembled.texts, assembled.personNames)
        val model = assembled.model.copy(maps = drawn, warnings = assembled.model.warnings + warnings)
        val bytes = CanonicalJson.bytes(model)
        if (bytes.size > properties.maxFileBytes) throw DraftFailure(Deliverables.FILE_TOO_LARGE, Deliverables.DRAFT_MODEL)
        records.patch(
            Deliverables.DELIVERABLE,
            id,
            mapOf(
                Deliverables.STATUS to Deliverables.RUNNING,
                Deliverables.GENERATED_AT to clock.instant().toString(),
                Deliverables.MODEL_SHA256 to CanonicalJson.sha256(bytes),
                Deliverables.DRAFT_REVISION to model.meta.draftRevision
            ),
            DeliverableGenerationJob.REASON
        )
        val author = assembled.model.meta.preparedBy
        return Frozen(model, bytes, template.bytes, author)
    }

    private class Template(
        val id: String?,
        val sha256: String,
        val bytes: ByteArray
    )

    // la plantilla más reciente de la clave (especificación de F8 §2.2.4), con su SHA-256 comprobado; sin clave, la base
    private suspend fun template(
        organizationId: UUID,
        key: String?
    ): Template {
        if (key == null) return Template(null, CanonicalJson.sha256(BaseTemplate.bytes), BaseTemplate.bytes)
        val latest =
            records.all(Deliverables.TEMPLATE, mapOf("template_key" to key)).maxByOrNull { it.createdAt ?: Instant.EPOCH }
                ?: throw DraftFailure(Deliverables.TEMPLATE_UNAVAILABLE)
        val descriptor = latest.attributes["file"] as? Map<*, *> ?: throw DraftFailure(Deliverables.TEMPLATE_UNAVAILABLE)
        val bytes = read(organizationId, descriptor, Deliverables.TEMPLATE_UNAVAILABLE)
        return Template(latest.id, descriptor["sha256"].toString(), bytes)
    }

    // los bytes de un archivo de wasichai-files como la plataforma, con su SHA-256 igual al del descriptor (UPS-31)
    private suspend fun read(
        organizationId: UUID,
        descriptor: Map<*, *>,
        failure: String
    ): ByteArray {
        val id = descriptor["id"]?.toString()?.let { runCatching { UUID.fromString(it) }.getOrNull() } ?: throw DraftFailure(failure)
        val read = stored.read(organizationId, id, properties.maxFileBytes)
        val bytes = (read as? StoredRead.Found)?.file?.bytes ?: throw DraftFailure(failure)
        if (CanonicalJson.sha256(bytes) != descriptor["sha256"]?.toString()) throw DraftFailure(failure)
        return bytes
    }

    // hubo una emisión después del borrador anterior: la letra vuelve a la primera (ASM-027)
    private suspend fun issuedAfter(
        id: UUID,
        since: Instant
    ): Boolean =
        records.count(
            Deliverables.VERSION,
            filters = mapOf(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE to id.toString()),
            criteria = listOf(Criteria.between("issued_at", since, clock.instant().plus(Duration.ofDays(1))))
        ) > 0

    // paso 3, desde lo congelado y vuelto a leer: la prueba de que draft_model basta para volver a generar (RQ-REP-026)
    private suspend fun generate(
        organizationId: UUID,
        frozen: Frozen
    ): List<DraftFile> {
        val model = CanonicalJson.read(frozen.bytes)
        val photos =
            model.photos.mapValues { (_, ref) ->
                read(organizationId, mapOf("id" to ref.file, "sha256" to ref.sha256), Deliverables.GENERATION_FAILED)
            }
        val code = model.meta.code
        val out = mutableListOf(DraftFile(Deliverables.DRAFT_MODEL, "$code-modelo.json", Deliverables.MODEL_TYPE, frozen.bytes))
        for (level in listOf(Disclosure.INTERNO, Disclosure.EXTERNO)) {
            val leveled = ReportDisclosure.apply(model, level)
            val levelPhotos = photos.filterKeys { it in leveled.photos.keys }
            val draft = writer.write(DocxWriter.Input(leveled, frozen.template, true, frozen.author, levelPhotos))
            val candidate = writer.write(DocxWriter.Input(leveled, frozen.template, false, frozen.author, levelPhotos))
            val draftPdf = pdf(draft, model, level, candidate = false)
            val candidatePdf = pdf(candidate, model, level, candidate = true)
            val name = "$code-${level.name.lowercase()}"
            if (level == Disclosure.INTERNO) {
                out += DraftFile(Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_DOCX, "$name-borrador.docx", Deliverables.DOCX_TYPE, draft)
                out += DraftFile(Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF, "$name-borrador.pdf", Deliverables.PDF_TYPE, draftPdf)
                out += DraftFile(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_DOCX, "$name.docx", Deliverables.DOCX_TYPE, candidate)
                out += DraftFile(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_PDF, "$name.pdf", Deliverables.PDF_TYPE, candidatePdf)
            } else {
                // en EXTERNO el DOCX es un paso intermedio: no se guarda (solo PDF fuera de INTERNO)
                out += DraftFile(Deliverables.DRAFT_EXTERNAL_PDF, "$name-borrador.pdf", Deliverables.PDF_TYPE, draftPdf)
                out += DraftFile(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_EXTERNAL_PDF, "$name.pdf", Deliverables.PDF_TYPE, candidatePdf)
            }
        }
        out.firstOrNull { it.bytes.size > properties.maxFileBytes }?.let { throw DraftFailure(Deliverables.FILE_TOO_LARGE, it.field) }
        return out
    }

    private suspend fun pdf(
        docx: ByteArray,
        model: ReportModel,
        level: Disclosure,
        candidate: Boolean
    ): ByteArray {
        val revision = if (candidate) model.meta.issueRevision else model.meta.draftRevision
        val bytes = pdf.convert(docx, PdfRequest(candidate, model.meta.title, model.meta.code, revision, null))
        if (PdfChecks.problems(bytes, candidate).isNotEmpty()) throw DraftFailure(Deliverables.PDF_CHECK_FAILED, level.name)
        return bytes
    }

    companion object {
        // draft_warnings: un aviso por línea, «CÓDIGO» o «CÓDIGO sección»
        fun text(warnings: List<ReportWarning>): String? = warnings.joinToString("\n") { listOfNotNull(it.code, it.section).joinToString(" ") }.ifEmpty { null }

        fun parse(raw: Any?): List<ReportWarning> =
            raw?.toString().orEmpty().lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }.map { line ->
                ReportWarning(line.substringBefore(' '), line.substringAfter(' ', "").ifEmpty { null })
            }
    }
}

// JOB-05 DeliverableGenerationJob (workflows.md, especificación de F8 §3.3): en una sola réplica (ClusterLock con la clave
// sgspe.deliverable), por cada organización con entregables (Organizations, con su slug para el esquema de revisiones), lee
// como la plataforma los entregables en draft u observed y toma los pendientes (DraftQueue), cada uno con su candado
// sgspe.deliverable:<id> (dos réplicas nunca generan el mismo) y de a uno. una organización que falla no detiene a las demás
@Component
class DeliverableGenerationJob(
    private val records: Records,
    private val organizations: Organizations,
    private val generation: DraftGeneration,
    private val locks: ClusterLock,
    private val properties: DeliverableProperties,
    private val clock: Clock
) {
    private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(javaClass)

    // los borradores intentados; null si otra réplica tenía el candado
    suspend fun runOnce(): Int? {
        val lease = locks.tryLock(Deliverables.JOB_LOCK) ?: return null
        return lease.use {
            var done = 0
            for ((organization, slug) in organizations.slugs(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE)) {
                try {
                    done += SweepTiming.timed(log, NAME, organization) { organization(organization, slug) }
                } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                    throw e
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    log.error("{}: falló la organización {}", NAME, organization, e)
                }
            }
            done
        }
    }

    private suspend fun organization(
        organization: UUID,
        slug: String
    ): Int {
        val now = clock.instant()
        val pending: List<RecordResponse> =
            records.asPlatform(organization, SOURCE) {
                records
                    .all(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, criteria = listOf(Criteria.inState(Deliverables.REQUESTABLE)))
                    .filter { DraftQueue.pending(it.attributes, it.updatedAt, now, properties.generation.staleAfter) }
            }
        var done = 0
        for (deliverable in pending) {
            val lease = locks.tryLock(Deliverables.lock(deliverable.id)) ?: continue
            lease.use { generation.run(organization, slug, UUID.fromString(deliverable.id)) }
            done++
        }
        return done
    }

    companion object {
        const val NAME = "JOB-05 DeliverableGenerationJob"
        const val SOURCE = "job:deliverable-generation"
        const val REASON = "borrador de entregable ($NAME)"
    }
}

// el reloj de JOB-05: cada sgspe.deliverable.generation.poll después de terminar la vuelta anterior; sgspe.jobs.enabled=false
// lo apaga (las pruebas llaman a runOnce)
@Component
@EnableScheduling
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = ["sgspe.jobs.enabled"], havingValue = "true", matchIfMissing = true)
class DeliverableGenerationSchedule(
    private val job: DeliverableGenerationJob
) {
    @Scheduled(fixedDelayString = "\${sgspe.deliverable.generation.poll:5s}")
    suspend fun poll() {
        job.runOnce()
    }
}
```

### Tarea 12: Descargas, rutas nativas de archivos y `export_log`

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/main/kotlin/sgspe/privacy/ExportLog.kt`
- Create: `src/main/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableContent.kt`
- Create: `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableDownloadsApiTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tareas 4 y 10 (`readyDraft`); `FileService.open` como la persona; `Permissions`.
- Produces: `GET /api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/draft/content?level=&format=`, `GET
  …/versions/{versionNumber}/content?format=`, las rutas nativas `GET /api/objects/deliverable/records/{id}/files/{field}` y
  `…/deliverable_version/…`, y las seis `POST` de subida con `403 FILE_WRITE_NOT_ALLOWED`; `ExportLog.deliverable(...)`,
  `ExportLog.ISSUE` y `ExportLog.DOWNLOAD`.

Decisión 16 y Review Focus 2. Las rutas nativas atienden también la descarga de un borrador como la propia (con su fila de
`export_log`). La de `deliverable_template` queda la de wasichai (READ). El plan 8.1 dejó anotado que `F2ApiTest.stagedFile`
subía por `POST /api/objects/{object}/files/{field}`: desde esta tarea esa ruta es `403` para los tres objetos, y las ayudas
de `F8ApiTest` ya suben con `FileService.stage` (`stage`); si `PermissionMatrixApiTest` arma el registro mínimo de un objeto
de entregables con `stagedFile`, se cambia a `stage(admin(), …)` en esta misma tarea.

`src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableDownloadsApiTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import org.springframework.http.MediaType
import org.springframework.http.client.MultipartBodyBuilder
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.BodyInserters
import sgspe.F8ApiTest

// security.md §14 («Descargas»), api.md §4.12, deliverables.md §13.2 criterios 14, 22, 23 y 24 (Review Focus 2): las rutas
// propias y las nativas sirven el borrador con la marca a quien tiene DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED y dejan su fila DESCARGA; las
// candidatas, el modelo y el DOCX fuera de INTERNO no se sirven; nada se sube por la API genérica
class DeliverableDownloadsApiTest : F8ApiTest() {
    private fun downloads(id: String): Int =
        tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/export_log/records?deliverable=$id&export_kind=DESCARGA", null, HttpStatus.OK))["content"].size()

    @Test
    fun `el borrador se baja con DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED, con ETag y su fila DESCARGA, por la ruta propia y la nativa`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val id = deliverable(project)
        readyDraft(id)
        val manager = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER", project)
        val pdf = exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft/content?level=INTERNO&format=pdf", token = manager.token)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.OK, pdf.status, pdf.body)
        assertEquals(record("deliverable", id)["attributes"][Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF]["sha256"].asString(), pdf.headers.eTag?.trim('"'))
        assertTrue(pdf.headers.contentDisposition.isAttachment)
        assertEquals("nosniff", pdf.headers.getFirst("X-Content-Type-Options"))
        assertEquals(
            HttpStatus.OK,
            exchange("GET", "/api/objects/deliverable/records/$id/files/${Deliverables.DRAFT_EXTERNAL_PDF}", token = manager.token).status
        )
        assertEquals(2, downloads(id))
        val reader = member("PROJECT_MANAGER", project)
        val denied = exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft/content?level=INTERNO&format=pdf", token = reader.token)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, denied.status, denied.body)
        assertEquals(2, downloads(id))
    }

    @Test
    fun `candidatas, modelo y DOCX fuera de INTERNO son 404 y un formato desconocido 422`() {
        val id = deliverable(testProject())
        readyDraft(id)
        for (field in listOf(
            Deliverables.DRAFT_MODEL,
            Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_DOCX,
            Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_PDF,
            Deliverables.CANDIDATE_EXTERNAL_PDF
        )) {
            assertEquals(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, exchange("GET", "/api/objects/deliverable/records/$id/files/$field").status, field)
        }
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft/content?level=EXTERNO&format=docx").status)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft/content?level=INTERNO&format=model").status)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/draft/content?level=INTERNO&format=odt").status)
        assertEquals(0, downloads(id))
    }

    @Test
    fun `una version EXTERNO se baja con EXPORT y una INTERNO con DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED, y su modelo nunca`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val id = deliverable(project)
        val internal = deliverableVersion(id, 1, "INTERNO")
        deliverableVersion(id, 2, "EXTERNO", derivedFrom = internal)
        val reader = member("PROJECT_MANAGER", project)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.OK, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/versions/2/content", token = reader.token).status)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/versions/1/content", token = reader.token).status)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/versions/2/content?format=docx").status)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.OK, exchange("GET", "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/versions/1/content?format=docx").status)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, exchange("GET", "/api/objects/deliverable_version/records/$internal/files/model").status)
    }

    @Test
    fun `subir o reemplazar un archivo de entregable por la API generica es 403 FILE_WRITE_NOT_ALLOWED`() {
        val id = deliverable(testProject())
        val parts = MultipartBodyBuilder().apply { part("file", PDF, MediaType.APPLICATION_PDF).filename("x.pdf") }.build()
        for (path in listOf(
            "/api/objects/deliverable/files/${Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF}",
            "/api/objects/deliverable/records/$id/files/${Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF}",
            "/api/objects/deliverable_version/files/pdf",
            "/api/objects/deliverable_template/files/file"
        )) {
            val body =
                client
                    .post()
                    .uri(path)
                    .header("Authorization", token)
                    .contentType(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
                    .body(BodyInserters.fromMultipartData(parts))
                    .exchange()
                    .expectStatus()
                    .isForbidden
                    .expectBody(String::class.java)
                    .returnResult()
                    .responseBody!!
            assertEquals("FILE_WRITE_NOT_ALLOWED", tree(body)["errors"][0]["message"].asString(), path)
        }
    }
}
```

```diff
     companion object {

     }
```

`src/main/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableContent.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBuffer
import org.springframework.http.CacheControl
import org.springframework.http.ContentDisposition
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import org.springframework.http.MediaType
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux
import sgspe.common.Callers
import sgspe.common.Finding
import sgspe.common.Records
import sgspe.common.StrictQuery
import sgspe.common.UnprocessableContentException
import sgspe.common.toCodedException
import sgspe.privacy.ExportLog
import wasichai.core.common.FieldViolation
import wasichai.core.common.NotFoundException
import wasichai.core.data.RecordResponse
import wasichai.files.FileService
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.UUID

// las descargas de un entregable (especificación de F8 §3.4, api.md §4.12, security.md §14): leen el registro como quien llama
// (404 si no lo lee), comprueban la acción (DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED para un borrador de cualquier nivel y una versión INTERNO;
// EXPORT para una versión EXTERNO; si no, 403 ACTION_REQUIRED), escriben la fila de export_log (DESCARGA) y transmiten el
// archivo con FileService.open como quien llama, attachment, nosniff, no-store y ETag igual al SHA-256, como EvidenceContent.
// el DOCX fuera de INTERNO y el modelo son 404 (existen, pero no se sirven), un formato o un nivel desconocido es 422. las
// candidatas no tienen ruta: nada sin la marca BORRADOR se sirve antes de emitir (RQ-REP-021)
@Service
class DeliverableContent(
    private val records: Records,
    private val callers: Callers,
    private val permissions: sgspe.common.Permissions,
    private val files: FileService,
    private val exportLog: ExportLog
) {
    suspend fun draft(
        id: UUID,
        level: String?,
        format: String?
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        val chosenLevel = level(level)
        val chosenFormat = format(format)
        val user = callers.require()
        val deliverable = records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id)
        val field =
            when {
                chosenFormat == MODEL -> throw notServed()
                chosenLevel == Deliverables.INTERNAL && chosenFormat == DOCX -> Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_DOCX
                chosenLevel == Deliverables.INTERNAL -> Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF
                chosenFormat == DOCX -> throw notServed()
                else -> Deliverables.DRAFT_EXTERNAL_PDF
            }
        require(user, Deliverables.DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED, "Descargar un borrador")
        if (deliverable.attributes[field] == null) throw notServed()
        exportLog.deliverable(
            user,
            ExportLog.DOWNLOAD,
            chosenLevel,
            chosenFormat,
            "borrador ${deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.DRAFT_REVISION]} congelado el ${deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.GENERATED_AT]}",
            project(deliverable),
            id,
            null
        )
        return stream(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id, field)
    }

    suspend fun version(
        id: UUID,
        number: Int,
        format: String?
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id)
        val version =
            records
                .all(Deliverables.VERSION, mapOf(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE to id.toString(), Deliverables.VERSION_NUMBER to number.toString()))
                .singleOrNull() ?: throw NotFoundException("La versión $number no existe o no está a su alcance")
        return version(version, format(format))
    }

    suspend fun versionById(
        versionId: UUID,
        field: String
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        val version = records.get(Deliverables.VERSION, versionId)
        val format =
            when (field) {
                Deliverables.PDF -> PDF
                Deliverables.DOCX -> DOCX
                else -> throw notServed()
            }
        return version(version, format)
    }

    private suspend fun version(
        version: RecordResponse,
        format: String
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        val user = callers.require()
        val level = version.attributes[Deliverables.LEVEL]?.toString()
        if (format == MODEL || (format == DOCX && level != Deliverables.INTERNAL)) throw notServed()
        if (level ==
            Deliverables.INTERNAL
        ) {
            require(user, Deliverables.DOWNLOAD_RESTRICTED, "Descargar una versión INTERNO")
        } else {
            require(user, Deliverables.EXPORT, "Descargar una versión $level")
        }
        val field = if (format == DOCX) Deliverables.DOCX else Deliverables.PDF
        if (version.attributes[field] == null) throw notServed()
        val deliverableId = UUID.fromString(version.attributes[Deliverables.DELIVERABLE].toString())
        val deliverable = records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, deliverableId)
        exportLog.deliverable(
            user,
            ExportLog.DOWNLOAD,
            level ?: "",
            format,
            "versión ${version.attributes[Deliverables.VERSION_NUMBER]} (revisión ${version.attributes["revision"]})",
            project(deliverable),
            null,
            UUID.fromString(version.id)
        )
        return stream(Deliverables.VERSION, UUID.fromString(version.id), field)
    }

    // la ruta nativa de un campo FILE de deliverable: los borradores, como GET …/draft/content; las candidatas, el modelo y lo
    // que no existe, 404
    suspend fun native(
        id: UUID,
        field: String
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> =
        when (field) {
            Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_DOCX -> draft(id, Deliverables.INTERNAL, DOCX)
            Deliverables.DRAFT_INTERNAL_PDF -> draft(id, Deliverables.INTERNAL, PDF)
            Deliverables.DRAFT_EXTERNAL_PDF -> draft(id, Deliverables.EXTERNAL, PDF)
            else -> throw notServed()
        }

    private suspend fun require(
        user: wasichai.core.identity.AuthenticatedUser,
        action: String,
        what: String
    ) {
        if (!permissions.has(user, action, Deliverables.DELIVERABLE)) {
            throw Finding("ACTION_REQUIRED", "$what exige la acción $action sobre deliverable", "record", status = HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN).toCodedException()
        }
    }

    private fun project(deliverable: RecordResponse): UUID? = deliverable.attributes[Deliverables.PROJECT]?.toString()?.let { UUID.fromString(it) }

    private suspend fun stream(
        objectName: String,
        id: UUID,
        field: String
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        val download = files.open(objectName, id, field)
        val file = download.file
        return ResponseEntity
            .ok()
            .contentType(MediaType.parseMediaType(file.contentType))
            .contentLength(file.sizeBytes)
            .eTag(file.sha256)
            .cacheControl(CacheControl.noStore())
            .header(
                HttpHeaders.CONTENT_DISPOSITION,
                ContentDisposition
                    .attachment()
                    .filename(file.fileName, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                    .build()
                    .toString()
            ).header(NOSNIFF, "nosniff")
            .body(download.content)
    }

    private fun level(raw: String?): String =
        when (raw) {
            Deliverables.INTERNAL, Deliverables.EXTERNAL -> raw
            else -> throw UnprocessableContentException(listOf(FieldViolation("level", "INTERNO o EXTERNO")))
        }

    private fun format(raw: String?): String =
        when (raw ?: PDF) {
            PDF, DOCX, MODEL -> raw ?: PDF
            else -> throw UnprocessableContentException(listOf(FieldViolation("format", "pdf o, solo en INTERNO, docx")))
        }

    private fun notServed(): NotFoundException = NotFoundException("Ese archivo del entregable no se sirve")

    companion object {
        const val PDF = "pdf"
        const val DOCX = "docx"
        const val MODEL = "model"
        const val NOSNIFF = "X-Content-Type-Options"
    }
}

// las rutas propias de descarga y las nativas de wasichai-files de los tres objetos (especificación de F8 §2.2.6): el
// segmento literal gana a {object}, así la API genérica no se salta export_log ni la marca de agua. la subida y el reemplazo
// por la API genérica, 403 FILE_WRITE_NOT_ALLOWED: los archivos de entregables los escriben JOB-05, la emisión y POST
// /api/sgspe/deliverable-templates. la descarga nativa de una plantilla queda como la de wasichai (READ)
@RestController
class DeliverableContentController(
    private val content: DeliverableContent
) {
    @GetMapping("/api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/draft/content")
    suspend fun draft(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        @RequestParam(required = false) level: String?,
        @RequestParam(required = false) format: String?,
        exchange: ServerWebExchange
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        StrictQuery.requireOnly(exchange.request.queryParams, setOf("level", "format"))
        return content.draft(id, level, format)
    }

    @GetMapping("/api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/versions/{versionNumber}/content")
    suspend fun version(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        @PathVariable versionNumber: Int,
        @RequestParam(required = false) format: String?,
        exchange: ServerWebExchange
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> {
        StrictQuery.requireOnly(exchange.request.queryParams, setOf("format"))
        return content.version(id, versionNumber, format)
    }

    @GetMapping("/api/objects/deliverable/records/{id}/files/{field}")
    suspend fun nativeDraft(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        @PathVariable field: String
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> = content.native(id, field)

    @GetMapping("/api/objects/deliverable_version/records/{id}/files/{field}")
    suspend fun nativeVersion(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        @PathVariable field: String
    ): ResponseEntity<Flux<DataBuffer>> = content.versionById(id, field)

    @PostMapping(
        "/api/objects/deliverable/files/{field}",
        "/api/objects/deliverable_version/files/{field}",
        "/api/objects/deliverable_template/files/{field}",
        "/api/objects/deliverable/records/{id}/files/{field}",
        "/api/objects/deliverable_version/records/{id}/files/{field}",
        "/api/objects/deliverable_template/records/{id}/files/{field}"
    )
    suspend fun upload(): Nothing =
        throw Finding(
            "FILE_WRITE_NOT_ALLOWED",
            "Los archivos de entregables y plantillas no se suben por la API genérica",
            "file",
            status = HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN
        ).toCodedException()
}
```

### Tarea 13: La emisión y AUT-12

**Files:**
- Create: `src/main/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableIssue.kt`
- Create: `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableIssueApiTest.kt`
- Delete: `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/IssueCertificateApiTest.kt`
- Modify: `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/F8ApiTest.kt` (`issue`), `src/test/kotlin/sgspe/workflow/WorkflowMatrixApiTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tareas 10 a 12 (`DeliverableWriteGuard.reviewed`, `DraftGeneration.parse`, `ExportLog.deliverable`).
- Produces: `POST /api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/issue` (`201 { deliverable, versions[] }`), `IssueResult`; en `F8ApiTest`,
  `issue(deliverable, account, sha, checklist)`.

Decisiones 9 y 14 y Review Focus 3. El orden es el de §2.2.5: comprobar, copiar (fuera de la transacción de la emisión),
y dentro del candado del entregable volver a comprobar, crear INTERNO y EXTERNO, apuntar `current_version` y
`current_revision`, escribir `export_log` y aplicar `issue` al final (`Records.transition` lleva la marca que deja pasar
`CommandTransitionGuard`). La revisión, el SHA-256 de la plantilla y `ai_assisted_sections` salen del modelo congelado
copiado, no de datos vivos. `WorkflowMatrixApiTest` pasa a extender `F8ApiTest`: `prepare` da el borrador LISTO a `submit_for_review`,
`resubmit`, `approve` e `issue`, y `perform` emite por el endpoint.

```kotlin
    // emite por POST /api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/issue como `account`, con motivo e If-Match
    protected fun issue(
        deliverable: String,
        account: Account,
        sha: String = record("deliverable", deliverable)["attributes"][Deliverables.MODEL_SHA256].asString(),
        checklist: Boolean = true
    ): Response =
        exchange(
            "POST",
            "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$deliverable/issue",
            mapOf("draftModelSha256" to sha, "checklistConfirmed" to checklist),
            account.token,
            mapOf(IfMatch.HEADER to "\"${record("deliverable", deliverable)["updatedAt"].asString()}\"") + withReason("emisión de prueba")
        )
```

`src/test/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableIssueApiTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import sgspe.F8ApiTest

// especificación de F8 §2.2.5 y §3.5, deliverables.md §8.4, workflows.md WF-H y AUT-12 (Review Focus 3): la emisión por su
// endpoint como la persona; los casos de IssueCertificateApiTest del plan 8.1 viven aquí desde 8.2b
class DeliverableIssueApiTest : F8ApiTest() {
    private fun codes(response: Response): List<String> = tree(response.body)["errors"].values().map { it["message"].asString() }

    private fun versions(id: String) =
        tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/deliverable_version/records?deliverable=$id&size=10", null, HttpStatus.OK))["content"].values()

    @Test
    fun `emitir crea INTERNO y EXTERNO con la misma revision, el SHA-256 del modelo, export_log EMISION y la constancia`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val manager = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER", project)
        val id = approvedDeliverable(project, manager)
        val sha = record("deliverable", id)["attributes"][Deliverables.MODEL_SHA256].asString()
        val response = issue(id, manager)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.CREATED, response.status, response.body)
        assertEquals("issued", state("deliverable", id))
        val all = versions(id)
        assertEquals(setOf("INTERNO", "EXTERNO"), all.map { it["attributes"]["disclosure_level"].asString() }.toSet())
        assertTrue(all.all { it["attributes"]["revision"].asString() == "0" && it["attributes"]["model_sha256"].asString() == sha })
        val internal = all.single { it["attributes"]["disclosure_level"].asString() == "INTERNO" }
        val external = all.single { it["attributes"]["disclosure_level"].asString() == "EXTERNO" }
        assertEquals(internal["id"].asString(), external["attributes"]["derived_from"].asString())
        assertTrue(external["attributes"]["docx"].isNull || external["attributes"]["docx"].isMissingNode)
        assertEquals(internal["id"].asString(), record("deliverable", id)["attributes"]["current_version"].asString())
        val logged = tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/export_log/records?export_kind=EMISION&size=50", null, HttpStatus.OK))["content"].values()
        assertEquals(2, logged.count { it["attributes"]["deliverable_version"]?.asString() in all.map { v -> v["id"].asString() } })
        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", awaitRun("deliverable", "aut-12-issue-certificate", id)["status"].asString())
        val certificate = tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/deliverable/records/$id/documents", null, HttpStatus.OK)).values().single()
        assertTrue(certificate["number"].asString().startsWith("CE-"), "$certificate")
        assertEquals("0", certificate["snapshot"]["values"]["current_revision"].asString())
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, edit("deliverable_version", internal["id"].asString(), mapOf("revision" to "9")).status)
    }

    @Test
    fun `otro SHA-256, un mapa fallido, una seccion cambiada o sin la lista no emiten nada`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val manager = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER", project)
        val id = approvedDeliverable(project, manager)
        assertEquals(listOf("MODEL_MISMATCH"), codes(issue(id, manager, sha = "0".repeat(64))))
        assertEquals(listOf("CHECKLIST_REQUIRED"), codes(issue(id, manager, checklist = false)))
        platformUpdate("deliverable", id, mapOf(Deliverables.WARNINGS to "MAP_FAILED portada"))
        assertEquals(listOf("MAP_FAILED"), codes(issue(id, manager)))
        platformUpdate("deliverable", id, mapOf(Deliverables.WARNINGS to null))
        edit("deliverable_section", sectionId(id, "conclusiones"), mapOf("content" to "Cambio después de aprobar."))
        val changed = issue(id, manager)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, changed.status, changed.body)
        assertEquals(listOf("SECTIONS_CHANGED"), codes(changed))
        assertEquals("approved", state("deliverable", id))
        assertTrue(versions(id).isEmpty())
        move("deliverable", id, "return_to_draft", manager.token, "corregir la sección")
        assertEquals("draft", state("deliverable", id))
    }

    @Test
    fun `sin ISSUE es 403, sin If-Match 428, y emitir por la API generica sigue siendo COMMAND_ONLY`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val id = approvedDeliverable(project)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, issue(id, member("PROJECT_MANAGER", project)).status)
        val noIfMatch =
            exchange(
                "POST",
                "/api/sgspe/deliverables/$id/issue",
                mapOf("draftModelSha256" to "x", "checklistConfirmed" to true),
                headers = withReason("emisión")
            )
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.PRECONDITION_REQUIRED, noIfMatch.status, noIfMatch.body)
        val generic = transition("deliverable", id, "issue", token, "emisión")
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, generic.status)
        assertEquals(listOf("COMMAND_ONLY"), codes(generic))
    }

    @Test
    fun `una revision nueva conserva las versiones y la constancia es el unico tipo de documento`() {
        val project = testProject()
        val manager = member("SOCIAL_MANAGER", project)
        val id = approvedDeliverable(project, manager)
        assertEquals(HttpStatus.CREATED, issue(id, manager).status)
        move("deliverable", id, "start_revision", manager.token, "revisión 1")
        assertEquals("draft", state("deliverable", id))
        assertEquals(2, versions(id).size)
        val types = tree(send("GET", "/api/objects/deliverable/document-types", null, HttpStatus.OK)).values()
        assertEquals(mapOf("deliverable-issue-certificate" to "CE"), types.associate { it["name"].asString() to it["prefix"].asString() })
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Paso 2: Implementar**

`src/main/kotlin/sgspe/deliverable/DeliverableIssue.kt`:

```kotlin
package sgspe.deliverable

import kotlinx.coroutines.reactive.awaitSingle
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBufferUtils
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DefaultDataBufferFactory
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController
import org.springframework.web.server.ServerWebExchange
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux
import sgspe.common.Callers
import sgspe.common.CommandAnswer
import sgspe.common.Finding
import sgspe.common.Idempotency
import sgspe.common.IdempotencyKey
import sgspe.common.IfMatch
import sgspe.common.Permissions
import sgspe.common.Records
import sgspe.common.StrictBody
import sgspe.common.StrictQuery
import sgspe.common.Transactions
import sgspe.common.Versions
import sgspe.common.toCodedException
import sgspe.privacy.CanonicalJson
import sgspe.privacy.ExportLog
import wasichai.core.common.Actions
import wasichai.core.common.ValidationException
import wasichai.core.data.ChangeReason
import wasichai.core.data.RecordResponse
import wasichai.files.FileService
import wasichai.files.FileUpload
import java.time.Clock
import java.util.UUID

data class IssueResult(
    val deliverable: RecordResponse,
    val versions: List<RecordResponse>
)

// la emisión (especificación de F8 §2.2.5 y §3.5, deliverables.md §8.4, api.md §4.12), en la petición de quien emite y nunca
// como la plataforma: no genera nada. exige UPDATE sobre deliverable, CREATE sobre deliverable_version y export_log y la
// acción ISSUE, el motivo e If-Match; verifica approved y el borrador LISTO (409 STATE_MISMATCH), el mismo
// draft_model_sha256 que se revisó (409 MODEL_MISMATCH), ningún mapa fallido (409 MAP_FAILED), ninguna sección cambiada (409
// SECTIONS_CHANGED) y la lista de verificación EXTERNO (400 CHECKLIST_REQUIRED). sube como la persona una copia de cada
// candidata y del modelo (FileService.stage) en una transacción propia, que se confirma aunque la emisión falle (las subidas sin
// adjuntar las borra StoredFileCleanup); y en una transacción con el candado del entregable crea la versión INTERNO (DOCX, PDF
// y modelo) y la EXTERNO (solo PDF, derived_from), apunta current_version y current_revision, escribe una fila de export_log
// EMISION por versión y aplica issue al final, con la marca de Records que deja pasar CommandTransitionGuard, para que AUT-12
// vea el registro completo
@Service
class DeliverableIssue(
    private val records: Records,
    private val callers: Callers,
    private val permissions: Permissions,
    private val transactions: Transactions,
    private val versions: Versions,
    private val guard: DeliverableWriteGuard,
    private val files: FileService,
    private val exportLog: ExportLog,
    private val clock: Clock
) {
    suspend fun issue(
        id: UUID,
        body: Map<String, Any?>,
        reason: String?,
        ifMatch: String?
    ): CommandAnswer {
        val expected = IfMatch.required(ifMatch)
        StrictBody.requireOnly(body, setOf(SHA, CHECKLIST))
        val sha = body[SHA] as? String ?: throw ValidationException("Falta el SHA-256 del modelo revisado", SHA, "envíe draftModelSha256")
        if (reason.isNullOrBlank()) throw ValidationException("Emitir exige un motivo", "reason", "envíe la cabecera X-Change-Reason")
        val user = callers.require()
        permissions.require(
            user,
            "Emitir un entregable",
            Actions.UPDATE to Deliverables.DELIVERABLE,
            Actions.CREATE to Deliverables.VERSION,
            Actions.CREATE to ExportLog.OBJECT,
            Deliverables.ISSUE to Deliverables.DELIVERABLE
        )
        val before = records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id)
        verify(id, before, sha, body[CHECKLIST] == true)
        // las copias, como la persona, antes de la transacción de la emisión (decisión 9 del plan 8.2b)
        val copies = transactions.independent { copies(id) }
        val model = CanonicalJson.read(copies.getValue(Deliverables.MODEL).bytes)
        val result =
            transactions.locked("${Deliverables.DELIVERABLE}:$id") {
                val current = records.get(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id)
                versions.check(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id, expected, current.updatedAt, "El entregable")
                verify(id, current, sha, body[CHECKLIST] == true)
                val number =
                    (
                        records.all(Deliverables.VERSION, mapOf(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE to id.toString())).maxOfOrNull {
                            (it.attributes[Deliverables.VERSION_NUMBER] as Number).toInt()
                        }
                            ?: 0
                    ) +
                        1
                val now = clock.instant().toString()
                val common =
                    mapOf(
                        Deliverables.DELIVERABLE to id.toString(),
                        "revision" to model.meta.issueRevision,
                        "issued_at" to now,
                        "issued_by_user_id" to user.userId.toString(),
                        "issued_by_user_email" to user.email,
                        "model_sha256" to sha,
                        "template_sha256" to model.meta.templateSha256,
                        "ai_assisted_sections" to model.aiAssistedSections.joinToString("\n").ifEmpty { null }
                    )
                val internal =
                    records.create(
                        Deliverables.VERSION,
                        common +
                            mapOf(
                                Deliverables.VERSION_NUMBER to number,
                                Deliverables.LEVEL to Deliverables.INTERNAL,
                                Deliverables.DOCX to copies.getValue(Deliverables.DOCX).id,
                                "docx_sha256" to copies.getValue(Deliverables.DOCX).sha256,
                                Deliverables.PDF to copies.getValue(INTERNAL_PDF).id,
                                "pdf_sha256" to copies.getValue(INTERNAL_PDF).sha256,
                                Deliverables.MODEL to copies.getValue(Deliverables.MODEL).id
                            ),
                        reason
                    )
                val external =
                    records.create(
                        Deliverables.VERSION,
                        common +
                            mapOf(
                                Deliverables.VERSION_NUMBER to number + 1,
                                Deliverables.LEVEL to Deliverables.EXTERNAL,
                                Deliverables.PDF to copies.getValue(EXTERNAL_PDF).id,
                                "pdf_sha256" to copies.getValue(EXTERNAL_PDF).sha256,
                                Deliverables.DERIVED_FROM to internal.id
                            ),
                        reason
                    )
                records.patch(
                    Deliverables.DELIVERABLE,
                    id,
                    mapOf(
                        Deliverables.CURRENT_VERSION to internal.id,
                        Deliverables.CURRENT_REVISION to model.meta.issueRevision
                    ),
                    reason
                )
                val project = current.attributes[Deliverables.PROJECT]?.toString()?.let { UUID.fromString(it) }
                for (version in listOf(internal, external)) {
                    val level = version.attributes[Deliverables.LEVEL].toString()
                    val confirmation = if (level == Deliverables.EXTERNAL) "; lista de verificación EXTERNO confirmada" else ""
                    exportLog.deliverable(
                        user,
                        ExportLog.ISSUE,
                        level,
                        if (level == Deliverables.INTERNAL) "docx, pdf" else "pdf",
                        "versión ${version.attributes[Deliverables.VERSION_NUMBER]}, revisión ${model.meta.issueRevision}, modelo $sha$confirmation",
                        project,
                        null,
                        UUID.fromString(version.id)
                    )
                }
                val issued = records.transition(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id, Deliverables.ISSUE_TRANSITION, reason)
                IssueResult(
                    issued,
                    listOf(records.get(Deliverables.VERSION, UUID.fromString(internal.id)), records.get(Deliverables.VERSION, UUID.fromString(external.id)))
                )
            }
        return CommandAnswer(HttpStatus.CREATED, result, result.deliverable.updatedAt, "/api/objects/${Deliverables.DELIVERABLE}/records/$id")
    }

    // las comprobaciones de la emisión, antes de subir nada y otra vez dentro del candado
    private suspend fun verify(
        id: UUID,
        deliverable: RecordResponse,
        sha: String,
        checklist: Boolean
    ) {
        val a = deliverable.attributes
        if (deliverable.state != Deliverables.APPROVED || a[Deliverables.STATUS]?.toString() != Deliverables.READY) {
            throw Finding("STATE_MISMATCH", "Se emite un entregable aprobado con su borrador LISTO", "record").toCodedException()
        }
        if (a[Deliverables.MODEL_SHA256]?.toString() != sha) {
            throw Finding("MODEL_MISMATCH", "El modelo del borrador no es el que se revisó", SHA).toCodedException()
        }
        if (DraftGeneration.parse(a[Deliverables.WARNINGS]).any { it.code == Deliverables.MAP_FAILED }) {
            throw Finding("MAP_FAILED", "Un mapa del borrador falló: pida otro borrador cuando GeoServer responda", "draft").toCodedException()
        }
        guard.reviewed(id, a)
        // T1 y T2 preparan EXTERNO (ASM-037)
        if (!checklist) {
            throw Finding(
                "CHECKLIST_REQUIRED",
                "Emitir el nivel EXTERNO exige confirmar la lista de verificación",
                CHECKLIST,
                status = HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST
            ).toCodedException()
        }
    }

    class Copy(
        val id: String,
        val sha256: String,
        val bytes: ByteArray
    )

    // cada candidata y el modelo, leídos y vueltos a subir como quien emite para su campo de deliverable_version
    private suspend fun copies(id: UUID): Map<String, Copy> =
        listOf(
            Triple(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_DOCX, Deliverables.DOCX, Deliverables.DOCX),
            Triple(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_INTERNAL_PDF, Deliverables.PDF, INTERNAL_PDF),
            Triple(Deliverables.CANDIDATE_EXTERNAL_PDF, Deliverables.PDF, EXTERNAL_PDF),
            Triple(Deliverables.DRAFT_MODEL, Deliverables.MODEL, Deliverables.MODEL)
        ).associate { (source, target, key) ->
            val download = files.open(Deliverables.DELIVERABLE, id, source)
            val joined = DataBufferUtils.join(download.content).awaitSingle()
            val bytes = ByteArray(joined.readableByteCount()).also { joined.read(it) }
            DataBufferUtils.release(joined)
            val staged =
                files.stage(
                    Deliverables.VERSION,
                    target,
                    FileUpload(download.file.fileName, download.file.contentType, Flux.just(DefaultDataBufferFactory.sharedInstance.wrap(bytes)))
                )
            key to Copy(staged.id.toString(), staged.sha256, bytes)
        }

    companion object {
        const val SHA = "draftModelSha256"
        const val CHECKLIST = "checklistConfirmed"
        private const val INTERNAL_PDF = "internal_pdf"
        private const val EXTERNAL_PDF = "external_pdf"
    }
}

// api.md §4.12: POST /api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/issue con IK, IM y CR → 201 { deliverable, versions[] }
@RestController
class DeliverableIssueController(
    private val issue: DeliverableIssue,
    private val idempotency: Idempotency
) {
    @PostMapping("/api/sgspe/deliverables/{id}/issue")
    suspend fun issue(
        @PathVariable id: UUID,
        @RequestBody body: Map<String, Any?>,
        @RequestHeader(name = ChangeReason.HEADER, required = false) reason: String?,
        @RequestHeader(name = IfMatch.HEADER, required = false) ifMatch: String?,
        @RequestHeader(name = IdempotencyKey.HEADER, required = false) key: String?,
        exchange: ServerWebExchange
    ): ResponseEntity<ByteArray> {
        StrictQuery.requireOnly(exchange.request.queryParams, emptySet())
        return idempotency.respond(key, "POST", exchange.request.path.value(), body) { issue.issue(id, body, ChangeReason.fromHeader(reason), ifMatch) }
    }
}
```
